Outlook                                                                      展望
Looking forward, the management of the Group believes that the               本集團管理層認為，本集團所處行業仍將面臨俄
industry in which the Group is operating will still face the impact          烏戰爭、能源危機、地緣政治影響經濟體等因素
of, among others, the Russia-Ukraine war, the energy crisis and the          影響，原輔料與能源供給價格會有一定波動，產
geopolitical influence on economies. The prices of raw and auxiliary         品需求可能收縮，同業競爭加劇。但隨著全球經
materials and energy supply will fluctuate to some extent, and the           濟發展的不斷變化和俄烏戰爭對供應鏈格局的調
product demand may shrink due to the intensified competition with            整，以及國內堅定不移穩增長的經濟基調，能源
other industry players. However, with the continuous changes in              開採與基礎設施建設的投資會穩定增長，這些因
the global economic development and the adjustment of the supply             素將對本集團產品需求產生積極作用。
chain pattern brought about by the Russia-Ukraine war, as well
as the unwavering theme of maintaining steady economic growth
in China, it is expected that the investment in energy mining and
infrastructure construction will increase steadily, which will have
positive impact on the demand for the Group’s products.

The Group will continue to promote various tasks regarding the               本集團繼續圍繞「專精特新」的產品策略定位推動
product strategy positioning of “specific, specialized, innovative           各項工作。堅持聚焦石油天然氣用管、新能源用
and new products (專精特新)”. The Group will focus on oil and gas                管及特殊無縫鋼管產品，著力產品創新、客戶創
pipes, new energy pipes and special seamless steel pipes, strengthen         新與市場創新；以滿足和引導客戶需求為目標，
product, customer and market innovation, meet and guide customer             打造獨有領先產品，提升產品的品牌效應；以全
demand, manufacture leading products with exclusivity, and enhance           面綠色發展為引領，推動生產經營環節的節能減

     Foreign exchange risk                                                     外匯風險
     A majority of the Group’s businesses are operated in the PRC and          本集團業務主要在中國經營，以人民幣計值，隨
     are denominated in RMB. With the growth of its export business,           著出口業務的增長，本集團通過遠期結售匯規避
     the Group hedges its exposure to exchange rate fluctuations               匯率波動的風險，本集團預期匯率變動不會對本
     through forward foreign exchange settlement and it is expected            集團產生重大不利影響。然而本集團會密切監察
     that the Group will not be subject to any materially adverse effects      金融市場將在必要時考慮採取適當的措施。
     arising from exchange rate fluctuation. Nevertheless, the Group will
     closely monitor the financial market and would consider appropriate
     measures as and when necessary.

     SHARE AWARD PLAN                                                          股份獎勵計劃
     The Company has adopted the Share Award Plans on 31 May 2022               本公司已於2022年5月31日採納股份獎勵計劃
     (the “Adoption Date”). The purposes of the Share Award Plans              （「採納日」）。股份獎勵計劃旨在(i)肯定及獎勵若
     are to (i) recognise and reward the contribution of certain eligible       干合資格參與者對本集團成長及發展作出的貢
     participants to the growth and development of the Group and to             獻，並向彼等提供激勵以挽留彼等繼續為本集團
     give incentives thereto in order to retain them for the continual          的營運及發展効力；及(ii)為本集團的進一步發展
     operation and development of the Group; and (ii) to attract suitable       吸引合適人才。
     personnel for further development of the Group.

     Pursuant to the Share Award Plans, the Share Incentive Committee          根據股份獎勵計劃，股份激勵委員會（於本報告

     select any eligible participant to participate in the Share Award Plans   者（不包括任何除外參與者）參與股份獎勵計劃，
     and make an award of Shares (the “Awards”) to them from time              並向彼等作出股份獎勵（「獎勵」）。關連人士股份
     to time. Eligible participants under the Connected Persons’ Share         獎勵計劃項下的合資格人員參與者為本集團的董
     Award Plan are directors (excluding all non-executive Directors           事（不包括所有非執行董事（包括獨立非執行董
     including independent non-executive Directors) and chief executives       事））及最高行政人員，而非關連人士股份獎勵計
     of the Group and eligible participants under the Non-Connected            劃項下的合資格人士參與者為本集團任何全職僱

     CORPORATE CULTURE AND STRATEGY                                            企業文化及策略
     Corporate Culture                                                         企業文化
     “Striving to be a first-class enterprise, serving first-class customers   「爭創一流企業，服務一流客戶，締造百年品牌」
     and building a century-old brand” is the vision and mission of the        是本集團的願景和使命，公司董事會及管理層緊
     Group. Closely focusing on to achieve this vision and mission, the        緊圍繞實現這一願景和使命，帶領全體員工始
     Board and the management of the Company lead all employees                終秉承「守法合規、誠實守信」的基本價值準則，
     to always adhere to the basic value principle of “compliance with         持續改善、不斷創新、勇於變革是公司長久發展
     laws and regulations, honesty and trustworthiness”. Continuous            的不竭動力。公司倡導思想上同心、目標上同
     improvement, uninterrupted innovation and courageous reform               向、行動上同步，以變應變，開放共享，始終追
     are the inexhaustible driving force for the long-term development         求「沒有最好，只有更好」。本集團將以穩健的成
     of the Company. The Company advocates concentric thinking,                長和優異的業績，實現股東、客戶、員工、供應
     common goals and simultaneous actions to respond to changes               商、社會的和諧發展。
     with openness and sharing, and always pursues “No Best, Only
     Better”. The Group will achieve harmonious development among
     shareholders, customers, employees, suppliers and the society with
     its steady growth and excellent performance.

     Corporate Strategy                                                        企業策略
     To achieve the goal the Company’s long-term development,                  為實現公司長期發展的價值目標，本集團專注於
     the Group focuses on its financial and ESG performance to                 在其財務及環境、社會及管治表現以實現可持續
     achieve sustainable development. The Group exercises stringent            發展。本集團執行於本集團收益、盈利、利潤及
     management over the Group’s revenue, earnings, margins and                成本，資本及投資回報及其他融資活動之嚴謹管
     costs, return on capital and investments and other financing              理。本集團於卓越運營、創新產品及技術基礎方
     activities. The Group’s core competitiveness in operational               面的核心競爭力為本集團於各行業獲取商業機遇
     excellence, product innovation and technology infrastructure is an        的重要舉措。近年來，本集團一直增加研發投資
     important measure for the Group to capture business opportunities         以促進創新及智能技術製造。亦致力保持穩健之
     in various industries. In recent years, the Group has been increasing     資金流動性及靈活性，維持長遠及平衡之債務償
     its investment in research and development to facilitate innovation       還狀況，並積極管理現金流及營運資金。本年報
     and smart technology manufacturing. The Group is also committed           中「主席報告」以及「管理層討論及分析」包括有關

ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT
環境、社會及管治報告

About this Report                                                       關於本報告
Dalipal Holdings Limited (hereinafter referred to as “the Company”      達力普控股有限公司（以下簡稱「本公司」或「我
or “we”) and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as “the         們」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）欣然提呈第四
Group”) is pleased to present its fourth Environmental, Social and      份環境、社會及管治（「ESG」）報告（「本報告」），
Governance (“ESG”) Report (the “Report”) to provide an overview         以概述影響本集團營運的重大事宜及管理情況，
of the Group’s management of significant issues affecting the           包括ESG事宜。
operation, including ESG issues.

Reporting Period                                                        報告期間
This Report describes the Group’s initiatives and performance           本報告說明本集團於2022年1月1日至2022年12
regarding the environmental and social aspects during the period        月31日止期間（「報告期間」）的環境及社會措施
from 1 January 2022 to 31 December 2022 (the “Reporting                 及表現。
Period”).

Report Scope                                                            報告範圍
Considering the materiality of revenue generated to the Group, this     考慮到所產生之收入對本集團之重要性，本報告
Report covers all subsidiaries of the Group in Hong Kong and Hebei      涵蓋本集團於香港及中華人民共和國（「中國」）河
Province of the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”), whose core      北省的所有附屬公司，其核心業務為主要從事
business is principally engaged in the development, manufacture         開發、製造及銷售石油工業專用管（「石油專用
and sale of Oil Country Tubular Goods (“OCTG”), new energy pipes        管」）、新能源用管及特殊無縫鋼管。本集團將持
and special seamless steel pipes. The Group will continuously assess    續評估其業務對主要環境、社會及管治層面的影
the impacts of its business on key ESG aspects, which are included in   響，並載入本報告。
this Report.

Reporting Basis                                                         報告準則
This Report is prepared in accordance with the Mandatory Disclosure     本報告依據香港聯交所證券主板上市規則（「上市
Requirements of the “Environmental, Social and Governance               規則」）附錄二十七所載之《環境、社會及管治報
Reporting Guide” (the “ESG Guide”) as set out in Appendix 27 to         告指引》（「環境、社會及管治報告指引」）強制披
the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the Main Board         露規定及其「不遵守就解釋」條文的披露責任要求
of the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”)        編製，目的是讓各持份者更了解本集團在營運及
and the “comply or explain” provisions contained therein. This          可持續發展的進程及發展方向。本集團認為屬重
is to enable stakeholders to better understand the progress and         大的若干關鍵績效指標於報告期間披露，並將繼
development direction in respect of the operation and sustainable       續改良及完善關鍵績效指標的披露。本報告最後
development of the Group. Specific key performance indicators           一章附有完整索引，以便讀者按環境、社會及管
(“KPIs”) that the Group considers to be material were disclosed         治報告指引閱讀本報告。
during the Reporting Period. The Group will continue to optimize
and improve the disclosure of KPIs. The final chapter of this
Report is accompanied by a complete index to facilitate readers’
understanding of the Report in accordance with the ESG Reporting
Guide.

About the Group                                                         關於本集團
The Group is mainly engaged in the research and development             本集團以石油專用管的研發與製造為主要業務，
(“R&D”) and manufacturing of OCTG, of which the business of             其中達力普石油專用管有限公司之業務始於
Dalipal Pipe Co., Ltd. began in 1998 and was headquartered in           1998年，總部位於中國河北滄州。本公司致力
Cangzhou, Hebei Province, China. The Group has been committed to        於研發、製造及提供一體化的專業服務、專注發
R&D, manufacturing and providing integrated professional services,      展成為一家石油專用管智能製造企業，於2019
focusing on the development of an intelligent manufacturing             年11月8日在香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交
enterprise for OCTG. The Group was listed on the Main Board of          所」）主板上市，以滿足未來業務的發展需求。
The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”)
on November 8, 2019, to meet future business development needs.

The Group attaches great importance to innovation-driven                本集團注重創新驅動發展，持續全面推行清潔生
development, continuously promotes cleaner production, green            產、綠色製造及智能服務，致力為石油能源產業
manufacturing and intelligent services, and strives to build core       及全球客戶創造更大價值，提升可持續發展能
competitiveness and sustainable development capacity, with special      力。為響應國家的「雙碳」目標，本集團積極通
pipelines for petroleum. In response to the national “Carbon Peaking    過制定科學的行動計劃，推動低碳轉型，力爭
and Neutrality Goals”, the Group actively promotes the low-carbon       在2030年實現碳達峰、2050年實現碳中和。在
transformation through the formulation of a science-based action        2022年，本集團成立董事會ESG委員會，並開展
plan, striving to achieve carbon peak by 2030 and carbon neutrality     「減碳行動實施方案」，在第六個五年規劃綱要中
by 2050. In 2022, the Board of Directors (the “Board”) of the Group     制定了圍繞「強、綠、智、新」的鮮明主題，聚焦
established the ESG Committee. It launched the “Carbon Reduction        「產品規劃、綠色發展、技術創新、管理創新、
Action Implementation Plan “and formulated a distinct theme of          智能製造、國際化經營」等六個發展重點以貫徹
“Strong, Green, Intelligent and Innovative” in the framework of the     落實我們的發展理念，為本公司未來五年的發展
sixth Five-Year Plan, focusing on six development priorities, such as   指明了戰略方向。
“product planning, green development, technological innovation,
management innovation, intelligent manufacturing and international
operation”, to implement our development philosophy, building the
strategic direction for the Company’s development in the next five
years.

The Group is committed to upholding business ethics and believes            本集團致力維護商業道德，並相信僱員誠實守信
that the integrity and commitment of its employees is one of the            為成功的關鍵因素之一。本集團嚴格遵守包括但
crucial factors to success. The Group strictly complies with relevant       不限於《中華人民共和國反不正當競爭法》、《中
laws and regulations, including but not limited to the Anti-Unfair          華人民共和國反洗錢法》、《中華人民共和國刑

和國反不正當競爭法》), the Anti-Money Laundering Law of the

the Prevention of Bribery Ordinance (《防止賄賂條例》) and other
national or regional laws and regulations.

     Whistle-blowing Policy                                                    舉報政策
     The Group sets up a whistle-blowing channel to encourage                  本集團設立舉報渠道，鼓勵僱員、供應商及客戶
     employees, suppliers and clients to raise concerns about fraud,           在保密情況下通過熱線、郵件、掃描二維碼及電
     corruption and bribery in confidence, through hotline, mail and           子媒介通訊向公司舉報舞弊、腐敗、賄賂以及其
     electronic media communications, as well as publicizes the reporting      他違反法律法規的行為，並在公司相關區域公示
     channels in relevant areas of the Company. During the Reporting           及張貼舉報渠道。於報告期間，我們推出「“碼”
     Period, we launched the “Report on Code, Reward Now” (「“碼”                上舉報，馬上獎勵」活動，並在公司內部渠道「達
     上舉報，馬上獎勵」) publicity, internally promoted the reporting                   力普人」公眾號宣傳舉報渠道，亦於企業微信內
     channel “Dalipal People” Official Account (“達力普人”公眾號),                    增加「反腐舉報」程序，提醒員工對不當行為、瀆
     established an “Anti-Corruption Whistle-blowing” program in               職或違規行為提高警覺，同時在公司網站及採購
     corporate WeCom to alert users towards suspected misconduct,              系統上設立了舉報熱線；供應商亦可通過留言的
     malpractice or irregularities, and set up a reporting hotline on the      方式在採購系統上舉報賄賂及違法行為。
     company’s website and procurement system for supplier’s whistle-
     blowing.

     All reported cases are promptly and thoroughly investigated by            所有舉報個案均由本集團之審計監察部實時及徹
     the Group’s internal audit department and reported to the Audit           底調查並就相關結果上報審核委員會及董事會。
     Committee and the Board. Where laws are violated, the case will           觸犯法律的，移送司法機關依法處理。倘有足夠
     be reported to the judiciary in accordance with the law. If there is      證據證明存在可能貪污行為，則有關個案會向相
     sufficient evidence to prove possible acts of corruption, the case will   關地方機關報告。我們禁止任何非法歧視或報復
     be reported to the relevant local authority. The Company prohibits        行為，或對於參與調查的員工採取敵對措施，並
     any unlawful discrimination or retaliation, or hostile measures           確保投訴、舉報人在協助調查工作中受到保護。
     against employees involved in the investigation. Complainants and         對違規泄露檢舉人員信息或對舉報人員採取打擊
     whistle-blowers are protected in assisting in investigations. Personnel   報復的人員，視為嚴重違反公司規章制度，將予
     who leak information on whistle-blowers or retaliate against whistle-     以撤職、解除勞動合同。
     blowers in violation of regulations shall be deemed to have seriously
     violated the Company’s regulations and will be dismissed and
     terminated from their employment contracts.

Anti-corruption training                                                      反貪污培訓
In terms of anti-corruption training, employees in sensitive positions        反貪污培訓方面，本集團安排敏感崗位員工至少
should be trained on anti-corruption at least quarterly, and other            每季度接受一次反貪污培訓，其他員工及單位至
employees and departments should be trained or organize a training            少每年接受或開展一次反貪污培訓。並舉辦各項
at least annually to promote the awareness of integrity. Internal             活動：
Audit Department organizes activities:

             • In order to strengthen employees’ awareness of integrity, during the Reporting Period, the Group formulated the
               “Integrity Education and Publicity Plan 2022”, while the Internal Audit Department launched the “Xiaofu's Blog”
               column, publishing integrity articles every month, and organizing various departments to learn as a warning.
     1       • 為加強員工廉潔意識，於報告期間，本集團制定《2022年度廉潔教育宣傳方案》，審計監察部推出「小付說法」欄
               目，每月發佈廉潔文章，組織各部門學習以做警示。


              • In conjunction with the “International Anti-Corruption Day” on 9 December, a thematic post was issued to launch a
                integrity initiative to employees.
              • 結合12月9日「國際反腐敗日」節日出具專題推文，向員工發起廉潔倡議。
     2

              • Organized thematic training on compliance management and loyalty and diligence obligations for directors and senior
                executives.
              • 組織董事、高級管理人員合規管理及忠實、勤勉義務專題培訓。
     3

              • Provided online and interactive anti-corruption training to directors, senior management and employees through our
                sustainability consultants, so that employees can learn in line with their own processes.
              • 我們亦透過可持續發展專業顧問，向各董事、高管、員工等提供線上及互動式反貪污培訓，令員工們能夠配合自己
     4          的進程學習。

     Stakeholder Engagement                                                 持份者參與
     The Group adheres to the enterprise value of “creating value for       本集團秉持「為客戶創造價值、為員工創造機

     for the enterprise and creating harmony for the society”. The          旨，而持份者的參與能讓本集團了解發展的風險
     stakeholder’s involvement enables the Group to understand the risks    和機遇，與持份者持續的溝通對於制定可持續發
     and opportunities of our development, ongoing communication            展戰略亦十分重要。我們識別了對本集團業務至
     with stakeholders is also very important for formulating sustainable   關重要的關鍵持份者，並透過各類溝通渠道以收
     development strategies. We have identified key stakeholders            集其意見，從而了解他們對本公司於可持續發展
     who are critical to the Group’s business and have gathered their       方面的預期，以制定各項措施回應持份者。
     views through various communication channels to understand

      Carbon Reduction Action Implementation Plan                          減碳行動實施方案
      At the beginning of 2022, the Group quickly launched the             2022年開始，本集團迅速啟動《減碳行動實施方
      preparation and promotion of the “Carbon Reduction Action            案》編製及推進工作。通過制定科學系統的碳達
      Implementation Plan”. By formulating scientific and systematic       峰、碳減排行動計劃，科學推動低碳轉型：
      carbon peaking and carbon emission reduction action plans, the
      Group scientifically promotes low-carbon transformation:



         Carbon Peaking, Carbon Reduction Action Plan
         碳達峰、碳減排行動計劃
         1.   Strengthen the top-level design of the Company’s system, establish a production and operation systems of
              green, low-carbon and cyclical development, improve resource and energy utilization efficiency, promote
              synergy in pollution reduction and carbon reduction, and create green and low-carbon products through
              equipment energy conservation and environmental protection upgrades, promotion and application of
              advanced technologies, continue to reduce carbon emission intensity.
              加強公司系統的頂層設計，建立綠色低碳循環發展的生產經營體系，通過裝備節能環保升級，先進技術推
              廣應用，提高資源能源利用效率，推進減污降碳協同，打造綠色低碳產品，持續降低碳排放強度。
         2.   Establish a sound carbon emission management system and strengthen the management of carbon assets.
              建立完善的碳排放管理體系，加強碳資產管理。
         3.   Track the R&D and application of cutting-edge technologies in low-carbon and carbon capture and carry out
              life cycle assessments and carbon label certification.
              跟蹤低碳與碳捕集前沿技術的研發與應用，開展生命周期評估和碳標籤認證工作。
         4.   Actively promote carbon emission verification and cleaner production review, promote clean energy
              substitution, improve the level of scrap resource recycling, and implement the all-scrap electric furnace
              technology.
              主動推進碳排放核查和清潔生產審核工作，促進清潔能源替代，提升廢鋼資源回收利用水準，推行全廢鋼
              電爐工藝。
         5.   Introduce advanced technologies in a timely manner, tap the potential of energy conservation and carbon
              reduction, promote the development of waste heat utilization, give full play to the function of energy
              conversion, and achieve coordinated carbon reduction.
              適時引進先進技術，深挖節能降碳潛力，推動餘熱利用發展，充分發揮能源轉換功能，實現協同減碳。
         6.   The Company intends to effectively improve the overall energy efficiency by focusing on the introduction
              of electric furnace waste heat power generation technology, scrap steel continuous charging preheating
              technology, amoeba residual steel monitoring technology, hydraulic servo energy-saving technology, etc. to
              strive to achieve the advanced level of cleaner production in energy consumption, material consumption and
              water consumption per unit product by 2025.
              公司擬通過重點引入電爐餘熱發電技術、廢鋼連續裝料預熱技術、阿米巴餘鋼監測技術、液壓伺服節能技
              術等，有效提高公司整體能效水準。力爭在2025年，單位產品能耗、物耗和水耗等達到清潔生產先進水準。

   Case Study: Forward-looking Cooperation, Promote Carbon Reduction
   案例：前瞻性合作，推動減碳
   During the Reporting Period, the Group and Air Liquide (Cangzhou) Co., Ltd. held the signing ceremony of
   the sustainable development project. The two companies adhered to the sustainable development concept
   of “low carbon, energy saving and environmental protection”, deepened cooperation and launched the
   “Continuous Casting Tundish Oxygen Combustion Innovation Project”, which adopts the oxy-combustion
   torrefying technology, and effectively improves the level of energy saving in production. It is predicted that after
   the implementation of the project, 50%-60% of gas energy will be saved, and carbon dioxide emissions will be
   effectively reduced.
   報告期內，我們與液化空氣（滄州）有限公司舉行可持續發展項目簽約儀式，雙方秉承「低碳、節能、環保」的可
   持續發展理念，深化合作，簽署並啟動了「連鑄中間包全氧燃燒創新項目」，該項目採用全氧燃燒烘烤技術，能
   夠有效提高生產節能水平。該項目實施後預計可實現50%-60%的燃氣節省量，並有效降低二氧化碳排放量。


   At the same time, the “biomass carbon utilization project” to be cooperated by the two sides focuses on
   the application of biomass carbon in steel mills , and realizes the full life cycle management and low-carbon
   metallurgical carbon footprint, which is the Company’s active exploration of the promotion of sustainable
   development with the energy transformation goals.
   與此同時，雙方擬合作的「生物質碳利用項目」，聚焦於生物質碳在鋼廠的應用，實現低碳冶金和碳足跡全生命
   週期管理，這是公司在能源轉型目標下推動可持續發展的積極探索。
108   Dalipal Holdings Limited 達力普控股有限公司


      ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT
      環境、社會及管治報告




          Reduce the Smelting Cycle of Billet Electric Arc Furnace and Reduce Emissions
          降低鑄坯電弧爐冶鍊周期，助力減排
          The high energy consumption of the electric arc furnace smelting is an important factor restricting the low-
          carbon green development of the entire iron and steel industry, and has long plagued the development and
          progress of enterprises. In view of this, in 2022, the Group thoroughly implemented the concept of “carbon
          peak and carbon neutrality”, formed an R&D team, and focused on the balance and coordination of “electricity-
          oxygen-iron” in the smelting process. The smelting cycle of the electric arc furnace has been shortened by
          10%, and the carbon emission content has been reduced by more than 10%. The successful implementation
          of this project has brought the Company’s overall green and low-carbon production level into a new stage and
          contributed to the national energy conservation strategy and the realization of the carbon peak goal in the short
          term, promoting the sustainable development of the steel industry, and laid the foundation for achieving a long-
          term green and low-carbon strategy.
          電弧爐冶鍊能耗大是制約整個鋼鐵冶金行業低碳綠色發展的重要影響因素，長久困擾着企業的發展與進步。針
          對於此，本集團於2022年深入貫徹落實「碳達峰、碳中和」理念，組成研發團隊，圍繞冶鍊過程「電-氧-鐵」的平
          衡和協同進行攻關，電弧爐的冶鍊周期縮短了10%，碳排放含量降低10%以上，此項目的成功實施將公司整體
          綠色低碳生產水平帶入了新的階段，對於國家能源節約戰略與短期內碳達峰目標的實現貢獻了力量，推動了鋼
          鐵行業可持續發展的腳步，為達成長期綠色低碳戰略奠定了基礎。

High-performance Shale Gas Drilling Used to Help the Construction of a 50 billion Large Gas
Area in the Southwest Oil and Gas Field
高性能頁岩氣鑽採用套管，助力西南油氣田500億大氣區建設
Shale gas refers to unconventional natural gas that occurs in organic-rich shale and its interlayers, mainly in
the form of adsorption or free, and is a clean and efficient energy source. Southwest China is rich in shale
gas reserves, and by 2025 a strategic atmospheric area of 50 billion cubic meters will be built. The geological
conditions of shale gas reservoirs are extremely harsh, and the required strength, toughness, and anti-collapse
performance of the casing are extremely high.
頁岩氣是指賦存於富有機質泥頁岩及其夾層中，以吸附或遊離態為主要存在方式的非常規天然氣，是一種清
潔、高效的能源。我國西南地區頁岩氣儲量豐富，2025年要建成500億立方米的戰略大氣區。頁岩氣藏地質條
件極端苛刻，對套管的強度、韌性、抗擠毀性能要求極高。


In response to the harsh geological conditions, drilling and production requirements of shale gas reservoirs,
the Group has carried out in-depth cooperation within the industry, academia, research and application, and
customized and developed a series of casing products for shale gas drilling with a yield strength of 1,000MPa.
It has the characteristics of “lightweight, high strength, high toughness, high collapse resistance, and high
connection strength”. By increasing the strength of the oil pipe and reducing the wall thickness of the pipe body,
the overall weight of the casing string is reduced by more than 30%, saving resources and reducing carbon
emissions by more than 20% during the manufacturing process.
本集團針對頁岩氣藏苛刻的地質條件和鑽採施工要求，進行產學研用深度合作，定製化開發了屈服強度
1,000MPa級頁岩氣鑽採用系列套管產品。具有「輕量化、高強度、高韌性、高抗擠毀、高連接強度」的特點，
通過提高石油管強度，降低管體壁厚，進而減輕了套管串的整體重量達30%以上，節約資源的同時，製造過程
降低碳排放20%以上。


In 2022, the Group supplied more than 15,000 tons of casings for high-performance shale gas drills, used
green products to facilitate the exploitation of green and clean energy, and contributed our strength to the
construction of a 50 billion large gas area in the Southwest oil and gas field.
2022年，本集團供貨高性能頁岩氣鑽採用套管15,000餘噸，實現了採用綠色產品助力綠色清潔能源的開採，為
西南油氣田500億大氣區建設貢獻了達力普力量。
110   Dalipal Holdings Limited 達力普控股有限公司


      ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE REPORT
      環境、社會及管治報告




          Case Study: Dalipal’s “Carbon Reduction Day”
          案例：設定「達力普減碳日」
          We actively implement the concept of low-carbon development, create a low-
          carbon atmosphere, and promote the low-carbon concept to employees. Since
          2022, we have designated World Environment Day on June 5 as Dalipal’s “Carbon
          Reduction Day” and launched a series of carbon reduction activities.
          我們積極實踐低碳發展理念、營造低碳氛圍，並向員工宣揚低碳理念，自2022年
          開始，我們將6月5日         「世界環境日」       定為  「達力普減碳日」         並開展系列       「減碳」 活動。


          We created a low-carbon and environmental protection awareness and
          atmosphere through hanging posters and online publicity activities. Everyone
          is encouraged to commute with low-carbon methods on their own, the air
          conditioners be turned off willingly in the office area, and promoted justified
          advice and activities about energy saving, consumption reduction, pollution
          reduction, and efficiency enhancement.
          通過懸掛海報、線上宣傳活動等營造低碳環保的意識和氛圍，倡議大家自發低碳出行、工作區域自發關閉空
          調，圍繞「節能、降耗、減污、增效」進行合理化建議和創新項目推廣活動等等。




      Greenhouse Gas Emissions                                            溫室氣體排放
      This Report focuses on scope 1 and scope 2 greenhouse gas           本報告集中披露範圍1及範圍2的溫室氣體排

        combustion technology on the combustion system;
        新生產線加熱爐使用潔凈能源天然氣為原料，燃燒系統採用低氮燃燒技術；

        增加2套揮發性有機物（「VOCs」）收集裝置；

        system), dynamically controlled the installation of online pollutant monitoring equipment for 3 spraying
        production lines system to ensure that the monitoring and measurement of pollutants are more accurate;
        and
        升級污染物監控系統（控制系統改為先進的DCS系統），對3條噴塗生產線污染物在線監測設備安裝動態管控
        系統，保障對污染物的監控測量更加精準；及
    •   Conduct weekly joint inspection of equipment, reduce equipment failure time, implement regular
        maintenance and preventive maintenance, implement equipment transformation and upgrading to
        continuously strengthen equipment maintenance management, and prevent running and dripping.

        強設備維護管理，防止跑冒滴漏。

Case Study: Scrap Metal Recycling via Electric Arc Furnace              案例：電弧爐廢金屬再利用
We are committed to establishing a green and low-carbon circular        我們致力建立綠色低碳循環發展的生產經營體
development production and operation system, such as the “Scrap         系，例如「電弧爐廢金屬再利用」循環系統。電
Metal Recycling via Electric Arc Furnace” circulation system. An        弧爐(EAF)為一種通過電弧放電加熱材料的加熱
electric arc furnace (EAF) is a heating furnace that heats a material   爐。生產過程主要是將廢金屬原材料加入EAF以
by an electric arc discharge. The production process is mainly to       進行冶煉，能提高能源使用效益，更能循環利用
add scrap metal raw materials to the EAF for smelting, which can        電弧爐排出廢金屬，作為原料重新投入生產線，
improve the efficiency of energy use. The system allows us to recycle   形成循環經濟以至促進了資源回收利用，令生產
the waste metal from the arc furnace, and re-enter the production       過程比起一般的線性生產模式更加環保，從而降
line as raw materials, forming a circular economy and further           低生產過程中的碳排放。
promoting resource recycling, making the production process more
environmentally friendly than the general linear production model,
thereby reducing the carbon emissions in the production process.

      Upgrade Equipment to Save Energy                                        裝備升級，助力節能


          Case Study: The Energy-Saving Renovation of Continuous Casting Hydraulic System
          案例：液壓伺服節能技術改造
          During the Reporting Period, the Group carried out a special action to improve energy efficiency around general-
          purpose energy-consuming equipment such as motors, and has taken the lead in implementing energy-saving
          transformation on casting hydraulic system, replaced the original pump sets with intelligent energy-saving pump
          sets, configured drive control devices, and introduced IoT services to achieve automatic control, online detection,
          intelligent correction and multiple modes are adopted.
          本集團於報告期間圍繞電機等通用設備開展能效提升專項行動，率先對連鑄液壓系統實施節能改造，將原有泵
          組替換為智能節能泵組，配置驅動控制裝置，並引入物聯網服務，實現了自動控制、在線檢測、智能校正多元
          化模式。


          At the same time, equipment managers can monitor equipment parameters at any time through the mobile
          cloud assistant and control the operation status of equipment. The energy-saving effect has been reduced from
          37.9 kWh/h to 12.8 kWh/h, a decrease of 66%, which can save more than 200,000 kWh of electricity every year.
          We also implemented the energy-saving transformation project of hydraulic presses. After the transformation, an
          average of 35 kWh of electricity was saved per hour, and the electricity saving rate was 42%, which achieved a
          significant energy-saving effect for the Group.
          同時，設備管理人員可通過手機雲助手隨時監控設備參數，把控設備運行狀況。 節能效果從37.9 kWh/h降至
          12.8 kWh/h，降幅達66%，改造項目預計每年可節電20萬度以上。我們亦實施水壓機節能改造項目，改造後平
          均每小時節省電能35千瓦時，節電率為42%,為本集團取得顯著節能效果。




          Case Study: Lifted Up the Pump’s for Energy-saving Renovation
          案例：提升泵節能改造
          1.   Energy-saving renovation of the hot water lift pump in the chemical degreasing room of the comprehensive
               pump room
               綜合泵房化學除油間熱水提升泵節能改造
               The Group started to analyze the operation principle of the hot water lift pump in the turbid ring water
               system from the current status of the equipment operation, fully studied the original design data of the
               system and the current real-time operation data, and measured the cooling tower, chemical degreaser and
               other related data in detail, and made a detailed analysis of the pump. Pump groups and pipelines were
               transformed. Compared with the original model, it is expected to save 2.28 million kWh per year.
               本集團從設備運行現狀着手分析熱水提升泵在濁環水系統內的運行原理，充分研究系統原設計數據與目前
               實時運行數據，詳細測量了冷卻塔、化學除油器等相關數據，對泵組、管線進行改造，相比原有模式，預
               計年可節約228萬度電。


          2.   Energy-saving renovation of the heat treatment advection pool lift pump
               熱處理平流池提升泵節能改造
               The heat treatment pump room has 4 installed self-priming lift pumps, and the rated power of a single
               pump motor is 160kW. After the transformation, the lift pump will operate in two-use and two-standby
               mode, which is expected to save 2.31 million kWh of electricity annually.
               熱處理泵房自吸提升泵裝機數量為4台，單台泵電機額定功率為160千瓦，改造後提升泵為二用二備運行模
               式，預計年可節約電量231萬度。

    Case Study: Regenerative Ladle Roaster
    案例：蓄熱式烤包器
    We replaced the original vertical ladle roaster with a heat-storage regenerative ladle roaster with a single-
    button operation, while reducing safety risks, improving heating efficiency, centralizing emission, reducing gas
    consumption by 46.6%, and annual gas savings of about 278,000 Nm3 base on the production time.

    我們將原有立式烤包器更換為蓄熱式烤包器，一鍵操作，降低安全風險的同時，提升加熱效率，集中排放，降
    低燃氣消耗46.6%，按生產時間計算年度節約燃氣約27.8萬標準立方米。

    Case Study: Reuse of Reclaimed Water
    案例：中水回用
    During the Reporting Period, the Group renovated its water system, interconnected drainage and reuse pipelines
    between production lines, improved the refinement of water system equipment management, improved the
    utilization rate of water resources, and reduced the amount of fresh water.
    報告期內本集團對水系統進行改造，將各產線間排水與回用水管線實現互聯互通，提高水系統設備管理精細化
    程度、水資源利用率提升，減少新水用量。

    In March 2022, the reclaimed water of the sewage treatment station was be reused, and the cumulative external
    discharge of reclaimed water in the annual sewage treatment station will be 2,505.75 m³. Compared with the
    same period in 2021, the annual discharge of reclaimed water decreased by 13,579.15 m³.
    2022年3月份開始污水處理站中水回用，年度污水處理站中水累計外排量2,505.75立方米。與2021年同期對
    比，年度中水外排量減少13,579.15立方米。

    During the Reporting Period, we completed the unattended weighing project and realized the automatic and
    paperless weighing of the incoming and outgoing process in the factory. In 2022, the electronic logistics system
    recorded about 7,500 weighing records, and the Jinzhong System printed about 80,000 papers. It is estimated
    that about 87,000 sheets of quadruple printing paper will be reduced each year after the project.
    報告期內我們完成無人值守稱重項目，實現了進出廠過磅自動化與無紙化。2022年電子物流系統過磅記錄約
    7,500張，金鐘系統打印約8萬張。該項目實現電子過磅單後，預計每年約減少8.7萬張四聯打印紙。


    The Company’s self-developed oil-casing pipeline thread data platform was launched. It is expected to reduce
    about 130,000 A4 papers per year. Optimizing the printing method of the exit pass, realizing the electronic exit
    pass, and cancelling the printing of the paper exit pass are implemented. Based on the data used in 2022, it is
    expected to reduce about 11,000 A4 sheets per year.
    公司自主開發的油套管線螺紋數據平台上線，預計每年約減少13萬張A4紙。優化出門證打印方式，實現電子
    出門證，取消紙質出門證打印。根據2022年已使用數據計算，預計每年約減少1.1萬張A4紙。

          Case Study: Clean Production Acceptance Inspection
          案例：清潔生產審核
          During the Reporting Period, the Clean Production Acceptance Inspection was carried out. The clean production
          management system was further improved, the management level and production technology level were
          improved, and certain environmental and economic benefits were achieved. Achieving electricity saving of about
          2,515,200 kWh/a, water saving of about 17,100 m3/a, natural gas saving of about 134,100 m3/a, and economic
          benefits of 2.6697 million RMB/a. At the same time, VOCs emissions are reduced by 1.31t/a, waste paint and
          paint residue are reduced by 0.1t/a, COD emissions are reduced by 0.306t/a, ammonia nitrogen emissions are
          reduced by 0.015t/a, and energy saving is 476.35tce/a.
          報告期內開展清潔生產審核工作，進一步完善清潔生產管理體系，提高管理水準和生產技術水準，並取得了一
          定的環境效益和經濟效益。實現節電約251.52萬kWh/a，節水約1.71萬m3/a，節約天然氣約13.41萬m3/a，獲得
          經濟效益人民幣266.97萬元/a。同時減少VOCs排放1.31t/a，減少廢漆、漆渣產生0.1t/a，COD減排0.306t/a，
          氨氮減排0.015t/a，實現節能量476.35tce/a。

          Online HR System
          線上HR系統
          In 2022, the Group successfully completed the smooth launch of the HR system and achieved the integrated
          management of business information such as recruitment, employee relations, leave, salary, training and
          learning, and employees’ daily information inquiries, automatic check-in, online learning, etc. On the premise of
          standardizing human resources management, a shared personnel information data platform will be established
          to improve the efficiency of information feedback and provide effective data support for company decision-
          making. At the same time, the process of using stamps for 7 offline forms and 9 transfer-in and transfer-out
          forms was optimized.
          於2022年，本集團圓滿完成了HR系統的順利上線，實現了招聘、員工關係、假勤、薪酬、培訓學習等業務信
          息一體化管理，員工日常信息查詢、自動打卡、在線學習等均可通過移動端完成，在規範人力資源管理工作的
          前提下，建立共享的人員信息數據平台，提高信息反饋的效率，為公司决策提供有效數據支撑。同時，優化了
          線下7個表單、9個入轉調離表單的用章流程。

Equal Opportunities and Diversity                                      平等機會及多元化
The Group adheres to diversified employment policies, promotes         本集團堅持多元化的用工政策，促進人才隊伍多
the diversification and rationalization of talent teams, and is        元化與合理化分佈，致力提供公平及平等的工作
committed to providing a fair and equitable workplace where            場所，使所有人就其於工作或僱傭的各方面獲平
all individuals are treated equally in every aspect of their work or   等對待。求職者及僱員擁有平等的就業、薪酬及
employment. Candidates and employees have equal opportunities          晉升機會。我們將不會以年齡、性別、種族、婚
for employment, remuneration and promotion. We will not conduct        姻狀況、宗教及傷健等任何受法律保障的理由或
or tolerate discrimination or harassment against individuals on the    任何其他我們認為不恰當及不可接受的理由對個
basis of age, gender, race, marital status, religion, healthy status   人進行歧視、騷擾或容忍有關情況。
or any legally protected ground or on any other ground which we
consider inappropriate and unacceptable.

We also place a high value on female employees’ career                 我們同樣重視女性員工的職業發展，確保女性員
development by ensuring that they have the same promotion              工擁有與男性平等晉升機會，並遵從《中華人民
opportunities as male staff. We also comply with the requirements      共和國婦女權益保障法》要求，女性員工不會因
of the Law on the Protection of Women’s Rights and Interests           懷孕、產假、哺乳等原因被無故解除或終止勞動
of the People’s Republic of China (“中華人民共和國婦女權益保                       合同並保證其獲得基本薪資的權利。
障法”), which stipulates that female employees are entitled to a
basic salary and their employment contracts will not be released or
terminated, without cause, during their pregnancy, maternity leave
or breastfeeding period.

Dismissal Policy                                                       解僱政策
With regard to the policy on the dismissal of employees, the           就有關解僱員工之政策而言，解僱程序亦必須符
dismissal procedure must be in accordance with applicable laws and     合適用法例及法規。我們將就解僱原因向員工清
regulations. The reasons for the dismissal will be conveyed to the     晰傳達，而員工將獲得足夠的提前通知，並在正
employee clearly. Enough notice would be delivered to employee,        式離職前需完成離職面談及所有的交接事宜，以
while resigning interview and hand-over procedure should be            保障員工的權益。倘員工之行為嚴重失當及其於
completed before leaving the Company to protect the employee’s         多次警告後仍未有改善，其主管及本公司高級管
rights. If an employee has committed serious misconduct and fails      理層則將進行全面的內部討論，並於宣佈解僱該
to improve in spite of repeated warnings, his/her supervisor and the   員工前讓其回應及解釋。
senior management of the Company will have a thorough internal
discussion and allow the employee to answer and explain before the
dismissal is announced.

Pandemic Prevention and Control                                        疫情防控
The Group adopted a series of prevention and control measures in       本集團按照政府要求採取了一系列防控措施，包
accordance with the requirements of the Government, including          括為員工提供口罩並專設一次性口罩丟棄垃圾
providing masks for employees and setting up disposable masks          桶、對廠房進行全面消毒，辦公區及住所場所每
garbage bins, comprehensively disinfecting the factory building,       日定時消毒、要求員工在食堂用餐時對角線就座
disinfecting office areas and residential premises on a daily basis,   以拉開就餐距離、門衛處設置安保人員24小時
requiring employees to sit diagonally during meals in the canteen,     值班以監督外來人員進入公司及員工乘坐班車之

          Learning Organization Establishment
          學習型組織建設
          According to the IDP Self-development Plan of the talent training project “Deep Blue Plan 2022” (深藍計劃2022), IDP
          learning and sharing community activities were carried out. The activities lasted for more than 9 months, with
          a total of more than 2,000 learning and sharing times, fully creating an environment of independent learning,
          mutual learning, and a joint learning atmosphere. After nearly a year of operation, the construction of learning
          organizations in the Company’s 14 units has basically taken shape, and 4 types of learning organization models
          have emerged, such as learning organizations that focus on learning, sharing, reflection, and review in various
          functional departments, and learning organization with problem-solving/topic research as the main focus. A
          learning organization that transforms downwards with IDP tools establishes a personal IDP self-development
          plan, and regularly organizes internal review and sharing; uses HR system learning modules to learn online
          courses, exam answering, and other functions are a learning organization built through activities such as “Learning
          Every Week” and “One Question A Day”.
          根據人才培養項目「深藍計劃2022」IDP自我發展計劃，開展IDP學習分享社群活動，活動歷時9個多月，累計學
          習分享超2,000次，充分營造了自主學習、相互學習、共同學習的組織氛圍。 通過近一年的運行，公司14個單
          位學習型組織建設基本成型，先後涌現出四類學習型組織模式，如以各職能部門為主學習、分享、反思、復盤
          類的學習型組織；以問題解決╱課題攻關類為主線的學習型組織；以IDP工具向下轉化，建立個人IDP自我發展

Talent Inventory Project
人才盤點項目
In 2022, the situation of middle-level and management high-potential personnel was systematically reviewed and
evaluated. First, through the investigation of the middle-level, we illustrated the Company’s middle-level and high-
potential talents from 4 dimensions of experience, potential, ability, and achievement, and provided a standard basis
for the training and selection of subsequent talents. Second, we organized the psychological assessment and 360
evaluation for the middle-level and management high-potential personnel, including 40 middle-level management
personnel and 21 management high-potential personnel, and 59 evaluated personnel participated in the 360
evaluation. At the same time, debriefing and talent calibration was carried out. Compared with the 2021 assessment
data, the ability improvement situation in 2022 was analyzed, and the ability improvement direction for 2023 will be
issued.
我們於2022年系統性盤點和評估了中層和管理高潛人員現狀。第一，通過對現有公司中層的調研，從有經驗、
有潛力、有能力、有成績四大維度輸出公司中層和高潛人才畫像，為後續人才的培養和選拔提供標準依據；第
二，對中層及管理高潛人員組織第二次心理測評和360評估，其中包括40名中層管理人員和21名管理高潛人員
心理測評，59名被評價對象參加360評估。同時開展述職答辯和人才校準，對比2021年度測評數據，分析2022
年能力提升情况，並出具2023年能力提升方向。

          Case Study: Green Economical High-toughness 110V Products
          案例：綠色經濟型高強韌110V產品
          In 2022, the price of alloys in the steel industry remained high. After a large number of literature research and field
          data analysis, the Group broke the traditional thinking of using high-cost Cr-Mo steel composition systems for high-
          strength and high-toughness oil casings and changed to adding economic alloy that combines strengthening elements
          and a special heat treatment process used to produce high-strength and tough oil casing products. The amount of
          alloy added in the same high-strength and tough products is reduced by 5%, which effectively reduces the use of
          alloy resources. During the Reporting Period, the delivery and use exceeded 4,600 tons, effectively reducing the energy
          consumption of each link in the national alloy mineral development process, and at the same time, reducing the
          natural gas consumption and electricity consumption in the smelting process after the composition adjustment, and
          contribute to the sustainable development of the country.
          2022年鋼業合金價格含量高居不下，經過大量文獻資料調研及現場數據分析，本集團打破傳統高強韌油套管一
          慣採用高成本Cr-Mo鋼成分體系的傳統思維，轉變為以添加經濟型合金強化元素與特殊熱處理工藝相結合的方
          式進行高強韌油套管產品生產，同等高強韌產品中合金添加量降低5%，有效減少了合金資源的使用，報告期
          內交付使用突破4,600噸，有效降低國家合金礦產開發過程中各環節的能源消耗，同時成分調整後冶鍊過程中
          減少了天然氣消耗及電能消耗，為國家可持續發展貢獻力量。




          Case Study: The First Launch of Rare Earth Corrosion-resistant Oil Casing
          案例：稀土耐腐蝕油套管實現首發
          Rare earth is known as “industrial vitamins”. Adding trace amounts of rare earth to steel
          can significantly improve the ductility, wear resistance, heat resistance, and corrosion
          resistance of steel. However, rare earth is reactive and difficult to add to the production
          process. The Group has cooperated with a well-known domestic university through
          industry-university-research cooperation. After nearly three years of joint research, in
          November 2022, the rare earth corrosion-resistant oil casing was first launched in China.
          Compared with conventional oil and casing products, rare earth corrosion-resistant oil and
          casing can improve the corrosion resistance by more than 50% without increasing the
          cost, which can greatly increase the life of oil and gas wells, reduce production costs, and
          improve the safety of petroleum engineering.
          稀土素有「工業維生素」的美稱，鋼中添加微量稀土能夠顯著提高鋼的韌塑性、耐磨性、耐熱性、抗蝕性等，但
          是稀土元素性質活躍，生產過程添加難度極大。本集團與國內某知名大學通過產學研合作，經過近三年的共同
          攻關，於2022年11月實現了稀土耐腐蝕油套管在國內首發。相對於常規油套管產品，稀土耐腐蝕油套管在不
          增加成本的條件下，耐腐蝕性能提升50%以上，可大幅度提升油氣井的壽命，降低開採成本，提升石油工程的
          安全性。

    Case Study: Heat-free Treatment of High-toughness Oil Casing Products
    案例：免熱處理高韌性油套管產品
    In order to improve the safety of oil and gas drilling and production, customers have
    higher requirements for the impact toughness of oil and casing, which can no longer
    meet the requirements according to the general API standards. To meet the customer’s
    requirements, according to the traditional process method, J55 and K55 steel-grade oil
    casings need to be produced by online normalization or offline full-length normalizing
    heat treatment, which has a long process flow and high energy consumption. As to
    meet the purpose of green research and development, the Group formed an R&D team,
    conducted a large number of tests from the alloy composition design of the material
    and the temperature control and deformation control of the rolling process, and finally
    successfully developed high-toughness J55 and K55 oil casings without heat treatment.
    The performance of the products has reached the production level of traditional online
    normalization or offline full-length normalizing heat treatment, which reduces carbon emissions by 25% compared
    with conventional production processes. In 2022, a total of heat-free high-toughness J55 and K55 oil casing products
    were sold to more than 5,000 tons.
    為了提升油氣鑽採的安全性，客戶對油套管的衝擊韌性要求越來越高，按照通用的API標準要求已經不能滿足
    需求。要滿足客戶的要求，按照傳統的工藝方法，J55、K55鋼級的油套管需採用在線常化或離線全長正火的熱
    處理方式生產，工序流程長、能源消耗高。 為契合綠色研發的宗旨，本集團組成研發團隊，從材料的合金成分
    設計以及軋制過程的溫度控制和變形控制進行了大量的試驗，最終成功開發出免熱處理的高韌性J55、K55油套
    管產品，各項性能均達到了傳統在線常化或離線全長正火的熱處理的生產水平，與常規生產工藝相比降低25%
    碳排放，2022年共銷售免熱處理高韌性J55、K55油套管產品5,000餘噸。

       Mandatory Disclosure Requirements
       強制披露規定

       Governance        A statement from the board containing the following elements:               Sustainability Governance
       Structure                                                                                     可持續發展管治
       管治架構              i.   a disclosure of the board’s oversight of ESG issues;
                         ii.  the board’s ESG management approach and strategy, including
                              the process used to evaluate, prioritize, and manage material
                              ESG-related issues (including risks to the issuer’s businesses); and
                         iii. how the board reviews progress made against ESG-related goals
                              and targets with an explanation of how they relate to the issuer’s
                              businesses
                         由董事會發出的聲明，當中載有下列內容：

                         (i) 披露董事會對環境、社會及管治事宜的監管；
                         (ii) 董事會的環境、社會及管治管理方針及策略，包括評估、優次
                               排列及管理重要的環境、社會及管治相關事宜（包括對發行人業
                               務的風險）的過程；及
                         (iii) 董事會如何按環境、社會及管治相關目標檢討進度、並解釋它
                               們如何與發行人業務有關連。

       Reporting         A description of, or an explanation on, the application of the              Reporting Principles
       Principles        Reporting Principles (Materiality, Quantitative and Consistency) in the     報告准則
       匯報原則              preparation of the ESG report.
                         描述或解釋在編備環境、社會及管治報告時如何應用匯報原則（重要
                         性、量化和一致性）。

       Reporting         A narrative explaining the reporting boundaries of the ESG report and       About this Report
       Boundary          describing the process used to identify which entities or operations        關於本報告
       匯報範圍              are included in the ESG report.
                         解釋環境、社會及管治報告的匯報範圍，及描述挑選哪些實體或業
                         務納入環境、社會及管治報告的過程。

a material misstatement of this other information, we are required      存在重大錯誤陳述，我們需要報告該事實。在這
to report that fact. We have nothing to report in this regard.          方面，我們沒有任何需要報告的事項。

Responsibilities of the directors for the                               董事就綜合財務報表須承擔的責任
consolidated financial statements
The directors are responsible for the preparation of the consolidated   董事須負責按照國際會計準則理事會頒佈的國際
financial statements that give a true and fair view in accordance       財務報告準則及香港公司條例的披露要求擬備真
with IFRSs issued by the IASB and the disclosure requirements of        實而中肯之綜合財務報表，並對其認為為使綜合
the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance and for such internal control         財務報表的擬備不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的
as the directors determine is necessary to enable the preparation       重大錯誤陳述所必需的內部監控負責。
of consolidated financial statements that are free from material
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.

and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors    非董事有意將 貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無

1 Corporate Information                                               1 公司資料

    in the Cayman Islands on 28 August 2018 as an exempted              月28日根據開曼群島法例第22章《公司法》
    company with limited liability under the Companies Law,            （1961年第3號法例，經綜合及修訂）於開曼
    Cap 22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the          群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司。本公司
    Cayman Islands. The Company’s shares were listed on the             股份於2019年11月8日（「上市日期」）在香港
    Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the          聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。
    “Stock Exchange”) on 8 November 2019 (the “Listing Date”).          本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主要從
    The Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are        事開發、製造及銷售石油天然氣用管（前稱
    principally engaged in the development, manufacture and sale        石油工業專用管產品）、新能源用管及特殊
    of oil and gas pipes (previously known as oil country tubular       無縫鋼管（前稱特殊無縫鋼管）以及其他產品
    goods), new energy pipes and special seamless steel pipes          （前稱管坯）。
    (previously known as special seamless steel pipes) and other
    products (previously known as pipe billets).

          (b) Basis of preparation of the financial statements                   (b) 財務報表編製基準
              The consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31          截至2022年12月31日止年度的綜合財
              December 2022 comprise the Group.                                    務報表包括本集團的財務報表。

              The measurement basis used in the preparation of the                 編製財務報表所使用的計量基準為歷史
              financial statements is the historical cost basis except that        成本基準，惟衍生金融工具按公平值列
              derivative financial instruments are stated at their fair value      賬除外（誠如附註2(e)載列的會計政策所
              as explained in the accounting policies set out in Note 2(e).        闡述）。

              The preparation of financial statements in conformity with           編製符合國際財務報告準則的財務報表
              IFRSs requires management to make judgements, estimates              需要管理層作出判斷、估計和假設，而
              and assumptions that affect the application of policies              該等判斷、估計和假設會影響政策的應
              and reported amounts of assets, liabilities, income and              用以及資產、負債、收入與支出的匯報
              expenses. The estimates and associated assumptions are               數額。該等估計及相關假設根據過往經
              based on historical experience and various other factors that        驗及在該等情況下認為屬合理的其他多
              are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances, the           項因素作出，其結果構成判斷不易從其
              results of which form the basis of making the judgements             他途徑得知的資產與負債賬面值的基
              about carrying values of assets and liabilities that are not         礎。實際結果可能有別於該等估計。
              readily apparent from other sources. Actual results may
              differ from these estimates.

              The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed                該等估計及相關假設按持續經營基準檢
              on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are           討。假如會計估計的修訂僅影響修訂作
              recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised            出期間，則於該期間確認；如有關修訂
              if the revision affects only that period, or in the period of        對當期及未來期間均有影響，則在有關
              the revision and future periods if the revision affects both         修訂作出期間及未來期間確認。
              current and future periods.

              Judgements made by management in the application of                  管理層於應用國際財務報告準則時所作
              IFRSs that have significant effect on the financial statements       出對財務報表有重大影響的判斷以及估
              and major sources of estimation uncertainty are discussed            計不確定因素的主要來源於附註3討論。
              in Note 3.

  (d) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                        (d) 附屬公司及非控股權益
     Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The Group            附屬公司指本集團控制的實體。當本集
     controls an entity when it is exposed, or has rights, to                團可以或有權從參與實體的業務分享非
     variable returns from its involvement with the entity and               固定回報，且有能力行使其對實體的權
     has the ability to affect those returns through its power               力影響該等回報時，本集團即對實體擁
     over the entity. When assessing whether the Group has                   有控制權。評估本集團是否擁有控制權
     power, only substantive rights (held by the Group and other             時，僅考慮（本集團及其他方持有的）實
     parties) are considered.                                                質權利。

     An investment in a subsidiary is consolidated into the                  於附屬公司的投資自控制權開始當日起
     consolidated financial statements from the date that control            至控制權終止當日期間於綜合財務報表
     commences until the date that control ceases. Intra-group               內綜合入賬。集團內公司間結餘、交易
     balances, transactions and cash flows and any unrealised                及現金流量以及集團內公司間交易產生
     profits arising from intra-group transactions are eliminated            的任何未變現溢利於編製綜合財務報表
     in full in preparing the consolidated financial statements.             時悉數抵銷。集團內公司間交易產生的
     Unrealised losses resulting from intra-group transactions are           未變現虧損以與未變現收益的相同方式
     eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains but only to              抵銷，惟僅限於沒有減值證據的情況。
     the extent that there is no evidence of impairment.

          (d) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                         (d) 附屬公司及非控股權益（續）
              (Continued)
              Non-controlling interests represent the equity in a subsidiary       非控股權益指並非直接或間接歸屬本公
              not attributable directly or indirectly to the Company, and in       司的附屬公司權益，且本集團並無就此
              respect of which the Group has not agreed any additional             與該等權益持有人協定任何額外條款，
              terms with the holders of those interests which would result         致使本集團整體須就該等權益擁有符合
              in the Group as a whole having a contractual obligation              金融負債定義的合約責任。就每項業務
              in respect of those interests that meets the definition of a         合併而言，本集團可選擇以公平值或按
              financial liability. For each business combination, the Group        於附屬公司之可識別資產淨值中應佔份
              can elect to measure any non-controlling interests either at         額，以計量非控股股東權益。
              fair value or at the non-controlling interests’ proportionate
              share of the subsidiary’s net identifiable assets.

              Non-controlling interests are presented in the consolidated          非控股權益於綜合財務狀況表的權益內
              statement of financial position within equity, separately            呈列，獨立於本公司權益股東應佔的權
              from equity attributable to the equity shareholders of               益。本集團業績中的非控股權益乃於綜
              the Company. Non-controlling interests in the results of             合損益及其他全面收益表列作在本公司
              the Group are presented on the face of the consolidated              非控股權益及權益股東之間的年內損益
              statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                  總額及全面收益總額之分配。
              income as an allocation of the total profit or loss and
              total comprehensive income for the year between non-
              controlling interests and the equity shareholders of the
              Company.

              Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that                倘本集團於附屬公司的權益變動不會致
              do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as              令喪失控制權，則作為股權交易入賬，
              equity transactions, whereby adjustments are made to                 而於綜合權益內的控股及非控股權益金
              the amounts of controlling and non-controlling interests             額會據此作出調整，以反映相關權益變
              within consolidated equity to reflect the change in relative         動，惟不會對商譽作出調整，亦不會確
              interests, but no adjustments are made to goodwill and no            認收益或虧損。
              gain or loss is recognised.

              When the Group loses control of a subsidiary, it is                  當本集團失去對附屬公司的控制權時，
              accounted for as a disposal of the entire interest in that           將按出售於該附屬公司的所有權益入
              subsidiary, with a resulting gain or loss being recognised in        賬，而所產生的收益或虧損於損益中確
              profit or loss. Any interest retained in that former subsidiary      認。任何在喪失控制權之日仍保留之該
              at the date when control is lost is recognised at fair value         前附屬公司股權按公平值確認，而此金
              and this amount is regarded as the fair value on initial             額被視為初步確認金融資產的公平值，
              recognition of a financial asset or, when appropriate, the           或（如適用）初步確認於聯營公司或合營
              cost on initial recognition of an investment in an associate         企業投資之成本。
              or joint venture.

              In the Company’s statement of financial position, an                 本公司的財務狀況表中，於附屬公司的
              investment in a subsidiary is stated at cost less impairment         投資按成本扣除減值虧損列賬（請參閱
              losses (see Note 2(i)).                                              附註2(i)）。

                                                                            （續）
  (e) Derivative financial instruments                                  (e) 衍生金融工具
     Derivative financial instruments are recognised at fair                 衍生金融工具按公平值確認。於各報告
     value. At the end of each reporting period the fair value               期末，會重新計量公平值。重新計量公
     is remeasured. The gain or loss on remeasurement to                     平值所產生的收益或虧損會即時於損益
     fair value is recognized immediately in profit or loss,                 內確認，除非衍生工具符合現金流量對
     except where the derivatives qualify for cash flow hedge                沖會計或海外業務淨投資對沖的條件，
     accounting or hedges of net investment in a foreign                     在該情況下，確認任何由此產生的收益
     operation, in which case recognition of any resultant gain              或虧損均取決於對沖項目的性質。
     or loss depends on the nature of the item being hedged.

  (f) Property, plant and equipment                                     (f) 物業、廠房及設備
     Property, plant and equipment are stated at cost less                   物業、廠房及設備按成本減累計折舊及
     accumulated depreciation and impairment losses (see Note                減值虧損列賬（請參閱附註2(i)）。
     2(i)).

     The cost of self-constructed items of property, plant and              自建物業、廠房及設備項目的成本包括
     equipment includes the cost of materials, direct labour, the           材料成本、直接人工、拆遷項目以及修
     initial estimate, where relevant, of the costs of dismantling          復項目所在地成本的初步估計（倘有關）

     production overheads and borrowing costs (see Note 2(u)).

     Items may be produced while bringing an item of property,               在將物業、廠房及設備項目運至可按管
     plant and equipment to the location and condition                       理層擬定的方式運作所需的地點及條件

     such items and the related costs are recognised in profit or
     loss.

         recognises a loss allowance equal to 12-month ECLs                        以等同12個月預期信貸損失金額確
         unless there has been a significant increase in credit                    認損失撥備，除非自初始確認後，
         risk of the financial instrument since initial recognition,               該金融工具的信貸風險大幅上升；
         in which case the loss allowance is measured at an                        在此情況下，損失撥備會以生命週
         amount equal to lifetime ECLs.                                            期預期信貸損失金額計量。

         Significant increases in credit risk                                      信貸風險大幅上升
         In assessing whether the credit risk of a financial                       評估金融工具的信貸風險自初始確
         instrument has increased significantly since initial                      認以來是否大幅上升時，本集團將
         recognition, the Group compares the risk of default                       報告日期及於初始確認日金融工具
         occurring on the financial instrument assessed at                         發生違約的風險評估進行對比。在
         the reporting date with that assessed at the date of                      進行重新評估時，本集團認為，倘
         initial recognition. In making this reassessment, the                     本集團不進行如變現抵押品（如有
         Group considers that a default event occurs when the                      持有）等行動，借款人便不可能全
         borrower is unlikely to pay its credit obligations to the                 額支付其對本集團的信貸責任即發
         Group in full, without recourse by the Group to actions                   生違約事件。本集團考慮合理及有
         such as realising security (if any is held). The Group                    證據支持的定量及定性資料，包括
         considers both quantitative and qualitative information                   過往經驗及無需付出過多成本或努
         that is reasonable and supportable, including historical                  力即可獲得的前瞻性資料。
         experience and forward-looking information that is

         Basis of calculation of interest income                                    利息收入計算基準

         impaired, in which case interest income is calculated                      下，利息收入按金融資產的攤銷成

         of the financial asset have occurred.                                       資產存在信貸減值。

         includes the following observable events:                                   以下可觀察事件：

         –    significant financial difficulties of the debtor;                      － 債務人出現重大財務困難；

              event;

              into bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation;                       他財務重組；

              legal environment that have an adverse effect on                         大變動而對債務人產生不利影
              the debtor; or                                                           響；或

              security because of financial difficulties of the                        失去活躍市場。
              issuer.

          – Calculation of recoverable amount                                     － 計算可收回金額
             The recoverable amount of an asset is the greater                      資產的可收回金額是其公平值
             of its fair value less costs of disposal and value in                  減出售成本與使用價值兩者中
             use. In assessing value in use, the estimated future                   的較高者。於評估使用價值
             cash flows are discounted to their present value                       時，估計未來現金流量將以反
             using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current                    映現行市場對貨幣時間價值及
             market assessments of time value of money and                          資產特定風險的評估的稅前貼
             the risks specific to the asset. Where an asset does                   現率貼現至現值。如資產並無
             not generate cash inflows largely independent of                       產生大體上的獨立於其他資產
             those from other assets, the recoverable amount                        的現金流入，則以獨立產生現
             is determined for the smallest group of assets                         金流入的最小資產組別（即現金
             that generates cash inflows independently (i.e. a                      產生單位）釐定可收回金額。倘
             cash-generating unit). A portion of the carrying                       可以合理及一致的基準進行分
             amount of a corporate asset (for example, head                         配，則公司資產（例如總部大
             office building) is allocated to an individual cash-                   樓）的一部分賬面值分配予個別
             generating unit if the allocation can be done on a                     現金產生單位，否則分配至最
             reasonable and consistent basis, or to the smallest                    小組別現金產生單位。
             group of cash-generating units if otherwise.

         –    Recognition of impairment losses                                    － 確認減值虧損
              An impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss                    當資產或其所屬現金產生單位
              if the carrying amount of an asset, or the cash-                      的賬面值高於其可收回金額
              generating unit to which it belongs, exceeds its                      時，於損益中確認減值虧損。
              recoverable amount. Impairment losses recognised                      就現金產生單位確認的減值虧
              in respect of cash-generating units are allocated                     損會按比例作出分配，以減少
              to reduce the carrying amount of the assets in                        該現金產生單位（或一組單位）
              the unit (or group of units) on a pro rata basis,                     的資產賬面值，但資產賬面值
              except that the carrying value of an asset will                       不會減至低於其個別公平值減
              not be reduced below its individual fair value less                   出售成本（如可計量）或其使用
              costs of disposal (if measurable) or value in use (if                 價值（如可釐定）。
              determinable).

         –    Reversals of impairment losses                                      － 撥回減值虧損
              An impairment loss is reversed if there has been                      如用於釐定可收回金額的估計
              a favourable change in the estimates used to                          出現有利變化，則撥回減值虧
              determine the recoverable amount.                                     損。

              A reversal of an impairment loss is limited to                          減值虧損撥回以資產於以往年
              the asset’s carrying amount that would have                             度並無確認減值虧損的情況下
              been determined had no impairment loss been                             的賬面值為限。減值虧損的撥
              recognised in prior years. Reversals of impairment                      回於確認撥回的年度計入損益。
              losses are credited to profit or loss in the year in
              which the reversals are recognised.

          (j) Inventories                                                         (j) 存貨
              Inventories are assets which are held for sale in the ordinary        存貨是指在日常業務過程中持作出售的
              course of business, in the process of production for such             資產、為銷售而處於生產過程中的在產

              in the production process.                                            料。

              Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net                  存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低
              realisable value.                                                     者列賬。

              Cost is calculated using the weighted average cost formula            成本採用加權平均成本公式計算，並包
              and comprises all costs of purchase, costs of conversion              括所有採購成本、轉換成本及為使存貨
              and other costs incurred in bringing the inventories to their         達至其現時地點及狀況所產生的其他成
              present location and condition.                                       本。

              Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the            可變現淨值為日常業務過程中的估計售
              ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of               價減估計完成成本及作出銷售估計所需
              completion and the estimated costs necessary to make the              的成本。
              sale.

              When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of those               存貨一經售出，其賬面值即於相關收入
              inventories is recognised as an expense in the period in              確認期間確認為開支。
              which the related revenue is recognised.

              realisable value and all losses of inventories are recognised as      存貨虧損均於撇減或虧損發生期間確認
              an expense in the period the write-down or loss occurs. The           為開支。任何存貨撇減的撥回金額於撥
              amount of any reversal of any write-down of inventories               回發生期間確認為存貨（已確認為開支）
              is recognised as a reduction in the amount of inventories             金額的扣減。
              recognised as an expense in the period in which the reversal
              occurs.

          (m) Cash and cash equivalents                                         (m) 現金及現金等價物
              Cash and cash equivalents comprise cash at bank and on              現金及現金等價物包括可隨時兌換為已
              hand, demand deposits with banks and other financial                知數額現金、其價值變動風險不大，且
              institutions, and short-term, highly liquid investments             自取得起三個月內到期之銀行及手頭現

  (r) Provisions and contingent liabilities                                (r) 撥備及或然負債
     Provisions are recognised when the Group has a legal or                    倘本集團須就已發生的事件承擔法定或
     constructive obligation arising as a result of a past event,               推定責任，且可能須付出經濟利益以償
     it is probable that an outflow of economic benefits will                   付有關責任及能可靠地估計有關金額
     be required to settle the obligation and a reliable estimate               時，則會確認撥備。倘貨幣時間價值重
     can be made. Where the time value of money is material,                    大，則撥備按預計償付有關責任所需開
     provisions are stated at the present value of the expenditure              支的現值列賬。
     expected to settle the obligation.

     Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                       倘可能無須付出經濟利益，或無法可靠
     benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be estimated               地估計有關金額，則將有關責任披露為
     reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a contingent liability,           或然負債，惟付出經濟利益的可能性極
     unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is                  低的情況則除外。就須視乎某項或多項
     remote. Possible obligations, whose existence will only be                 未來事件是否發生方能確定是否存在的
     confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one                       可能責任而言，其會披露為或然負債，
     or more future events are also disclosed as contingent                     惟付出經濟利益的可能性極低的情況則
     liabilities unless the probability of outflow of economic                  除外。
     benefits is remote.

     Where some or all of the expenditure required to settle                    倘結算撥備所需的部分或全部支出預期
     a provision is expected to be reimbursed by another                        由另一方償還，則就基本確定的任何預
     party, a separate asset is recognised for any expected                     期報銷確認單項資產。就報銷確認的金
     reimbursement that would be virtually certain. The amount                  額僅限於撥備的賬面值。
     recognised for the reimbursement is limited to the carrying
     amount of the provision.

     sales taxes and is after deduction of any trade discounts.

              its intended use or sale are interrupted or complete.

   (a) Movements in components of equity                                                                             (a) 權益部分的變動
      The reconciliation between the opening and closing                                                                  本集團綜合權益各部分的期初及期末結

      24 Financial Risk Management and Fair Values                              24 財務風險管理及金融工具公平值
         of Financial Instruments
          Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks        本集團在日常業務過程中面臨信貸、流動資
          arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.                   金、利率及貨幣風險。

          The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk             本集團面臨的該等風險及本集團用於管理該
          management policies and practices used by the Group to                 等風險的財務風險管理政策及慣例載述如
          manage these risks are described below.                                下。

          (a) Credit risk                                                        (a) 信貸風險
              Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default      信貸風險指對手方不履行合約責任而導
              on its contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to      致本集團承受財務虧損的風險。本集團
              the Group. The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable         的信貸風險主要來自貿易及其他應收款
              to trade and other receivables. The Group’s exposure to              項。本集團所承受來自現金及現金等價
              credit risk arising from cash and cash equivalents, bills            物、應收票據及衍生金融資產的信貸風
              receivables and derivative financial assets is limited because       險有限，原因是對手方為本集團管理層
              the counterparties are banks and financial institutions with         所指定具有良好信譽的銀行及金融機
              a high credit standing assigned by the management of the             構，就此而言，本集團認為信貸風險較
              Group, for which the Group considers to have low credit              低。
              risk.

              Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項
              The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by          本集團面臨的信貸風險主要受各客戶的
              the individual characteristics of each customer rather than          個別情況所影響，而非客戶經營所在的
              the industry in which the customers operate and therefore            行業，因此，重大集中信貸風險主要於
              significant concentrations of credit risk primarily arise when       本集團就個別客戶面臨重大風險時產
              the Group has significant exposure to individual customers.          生。於報告期末，貿易應收款項總額
              At the end of the reporting period, 16.9% (2021: 13.3%)              中 分 別 有 16.9%（ 2021 年：13.3% ）及
              and 55.7% (2021: 54.4%) of the total trade receivables               55.7%（2021年：54.4%）乃應收本集團
              was due from the Group’s largest trade debtor and the                最大貿易債務人及應收本集團五大貿易
              Group’s five largest trade debtors, respectively.                    債務人的賬款。

              Individual credit evaluations are performed on all customers         本集團對所有要求超過一定金額信貸的
              requiring credit over a certain amount. These evaluations            客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評估集中
              focus on the customer’s past history of making payments              於客戶過往繳付到期款項之記錄及現時
              when due and current ability to pay, and take into account           付款的能力，並會考慮客戶的具體資料
              information specific to the customer as well as pertaining to        及與客戶營運所在地的經濟環境有關的
              the economic environment in which the customer operates.             資料。貿易應收款項一般即時及最長於
              Trade receivables are generally due immediately and up to            發票日期後90日到期。本集團一般不收
              90 days from the date of billings. Normally, the Group does          取客戶的抵押品。
              not obtain collateral from customers.

              The Group measures loss allowances for trade receivables             本集團按相等於生命週期預期信貸損失
              at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs, which is calculated             的金額計量貿易應收款項的虧損撥備，
              using a provision matrix.                                            乃採用撥備矩陣計算。

24 Financial Risk Management and Fair Values                        24 財務風險管理及金融工具公平值
   of Financial Instruments (Continued)                               （續）
  (a) Credit risk (Continued)                                           (a) 信貸風險（續）

                                 at the measurement date                                      活躍市場的未經

                                 Unobservable inputs are inputs                               用重大不可觀察

                                                                                              的公平值
